# a-run-keeps-its-journal

- State: parked
- Priority: 1
- Sequence: 69
- Risk: severity=2 novelty=2 exposure=1 accumulation=1 basis="adds a steward tick pass, alerts and a retro launch on every seat; a wrong pass floods alerts or asks, or launches the retro too often"
- Tier: 2
- Intent: Every run of a goal keeps one readable journal that matches or beats the steward's hand journal of 2026-10-23: per step what was expected, what happened, the cause, the actor, and CARRIED when a person or the steward did the machinery's step; a stall is recorded once when an expected step misses its bound, even when nobody looks; the retro actually fires, reads journals and receipts, and proposes changes for Wido's veto, every CARRIED event a goal candidate; one verb reads it all (summary, --verbose, --json). Design draft by Fable: /Users/wido/agentic-tools-evidence/lane-test-20261009/designs/a-run-keeps-its-journal.md (5 units, ~780 lines).
- Origin: human
- Next step: Astra critique of the draft under the rare-case rule (at most three rounds), Wido's answers to its Q1-Q4, accept the page on main, then build U1 (journal package and verb) first
- OpenedAt: 2026-10-23T07:06:42Z
- Revision: 125
- Budget: elapsedLimit=4h attemptLimit=6 reservedJobMinutesLimit=720 activeJobLimit=1 reviewRoundLimit=20
- BudgetExceptions: 0
- Approved: by=human:Wido at=2026-10-23T07:06:58Z revision=2 opid=KGBS5RJXK9D5X0PFNSFNM6X3V9-m1e-c951e3fd authority=proven digest=8a8864ac911ed488687d463f54e155b03386b7b389e23eac2ad067b298cc5cb2 episode=2
- Parked: by=human:Wido at=2026-10-23T22:35:21Z because=parked in Wido's name (decision 47e): design-first goals wait until the notices land; stale lane goals wait for their turn
